// The routes /stats/detail may name. A path outside this list is counted as
// 'other': a crawler guessing at /api/… must not be able to invent names.
const COUNTED_API = new Set([...Object.keys(REST_TOOLS), '/api/wallet', '/api/pool-scan', '/api/preflight', '/api/fee-tiers',
  '/api/range-plan', '/api/best-route', '/api/total-supply', '/api/circulating-supply', '/api/nft/state']);

// THE REST TOOLS FOR A BOT, FROM THE TOOLS THEMSELVES (2026-11-04, operator: "trading bots"). /api/ listed bare names and
// nothing described a call: a bot builder had to read llms.txt or speak MCP. Each trading route here takes its
// parameters and its summary from the MCP tool it runs (MCP_TOOLS), so /api/ and /openapi.json cannot drift from the
// tools. The example is a real call (CAKE) that answers.
const CAKE = '0x0e09fabb73bd3ade0a17ecc321fd13a19e81ce82';
// [path, tool, example query, one line a bot builder reads first] — the long description stays in openapi.json
const BOT_ROUTES = [
  ['/api/preflight', 'bsc_token_preflight', `address=${CAKE}&usd=250`, 'Before every buy: can you get in and out at your size, what the round trip costs with the measured tax, who controls the contract. Branch on gate: stop | weigh | no_known_stop.'],
  ['/api/pool-scan', 'bsc_pool_scan', `address=${CAKE}`, 'The full read behind the preflight: cost per size, 1% depth both ways, measured tax, sell simulation, LP custody, holders, flow, every venue.'],
  ['/api/best-route', 'pancakeswap_best_route', `address=${CAKE}&usd=250`, 'Which PancakeSwap pool returns the most at your size (quoted by the venue), every other route, the round trip with the tax, the slippage it needs.'],
  ['/api/fee-tiers', 'pancakeswap_fee_tiers', `address=${CAKE}`, 'For liquidity: which fee tier of a pair actually pays, fees per $1,000 over all capital and over the capital within 2% of the price.'],
  ['/api/range-plan', 'pancakeswap_range_plan', `address=${CAKE}&capitalUsd=1000&quote=BNB`, 'For a V3 position: each range width replayed through the real swaps of the last hour — fees, time in range, exits. No forecast.'],
];
const toolOf = (name) => MCP_TOOLS.find((t) => t.name === name);
const botParams = (name) => Object.entries(toolOf(name)?.inputSchema?.properties || {}).filter(([k]) => k !== 'scan_pass')
  .map(([k, v]) => ({ name: k, required: (toolOf(name)?.inputSchema?.required || []).includes(k), type: v.type, about: v.description }));
const BOT_LIMITS = {
  key: 'none — no key, no sign-up; CORS open',
  free: `every route free; /api/pool-scan alone counts: ${SCAN_FREE_PER_DAY.toLocaleString('en-US')} per caller and UTC day, past that HTTP 402 or 429 with code free_limit`,
  day_pass: `past the free pool scans: POST ${SCAN_PASS_URL} — 0.50 USD1, half that in $BOBAI, free for a wallet holding 1,000,000 $BOBAI; then send header X-Scan-Pass (${SCAN_PASS_CAP.toLocaleString('en-US')} scans a pass)`,
  cache: 'pool figures are never cached (Cache-Control: no-store); GoPlus contract flags and holder lists up to 6 h',
};
function apiIndex() {
  return {
    what: 'Free REST tools for trading bots on BNB Smart Chain: measure a token before you trade it — can you get in, can you get out, what the round trip costs at your size. Every route is a GET and answers JSON.',
    start: `Before every buy: GET https://brainonbnb.com/api/preflight?address=<token>&usd=<your size> and branch on gate: "stop" (do not trade, stop[] says why), "weigh" (caution[] with figures), "no_known_stop".`,
    limits: BOT_LIMITS,
    trading: BOT_ROUTES.map(([path, tool, ex, does]) => ({ path, does, params: botParams(tool), example: `https://brainonbnb.com${path}?${ex}`, mcp_tool: tool })),
    bobai: [...COUNTED_API].filter((r) => !BOT_ROUTES.some(([x]) => x === r)).sort(),
    openapi: 'https://brainonbnb.com/openapi.json',
    alarm: 'Hold a token? bsc_rug_watch POSTs your webhook when it turns dangerous (one watch free): https://agent.brainonbnb.com/rug-watch',
    docs: 'https://brainonbnb.com/llms.txt',
    mcp: 'https://brainonbnb.com/mcp',
  };
}
function openApi() {
  const paths = {};
  for (const [path, tool, ex, does] of BOT_ROUTES) {
    const t = toolOf(tool);
    paths[path] = { get: {
      operationId: tool, summary: does, description: String(t?.description || '').slice(0, 1500),
      parameters: [...botParams(tool).map((x) => ({ name: x.name, in: 'query', required: x.required, description: x.about, schema: { type: x.type === 'number' ? 'number' : 'string' } })),
        ...(tool === 'bsc_pool_scan' ? [{ name: 'X-Scan-Pass', in: 'header', required: false, description: `Only past the free ${SCAN_FREE_PER_DAY} scans a day: a day pass from POST ${SCAN_PASS_URL}`, schema: { type: 'string' } }] : [])],
      responses: { 200: { description: 'The measurement, JSON', content: { 'application/json': { schema: { type: 'object' } } } }, 400: { description: 'A refusal, JSON with error, code (bad_address, not_a_token, is_wallet, bad_usd …) and hint' }, ...(tool === 'bsc_pool_scan' ? { 402: { description: 'Free pool scans for the day used: code free_limit, with the day-pass terms' }, 429: { description: 'Free pool scans for the day used' } } : {}) },
      'x-example': `https://brainonbnb.com${path}?${ex}`,
    } };
  }
  return { openapi: '3.1.0', info: { title: 'Brain on BNB — free trading tools for BNB Smart Chain', version: '1.0.0', description: `Measure a BSC token before a trade: honeypot and rug-pull read, round trip with the measured transfer tax, best PancakeSwap route at your size, fee tiers, range replay. No key. ${BOT_LIMITS.free}. Day pass: ${BOT_LIMITS.day_pass}. Measurement, not advice.`, contact: { url: 'https://brainonbnb.com/scanner' } }, servers: [{ url: 'https://brainonbnb.com' }], paths };
}

// Where a buy click may say it came from: the pages (scripts/deploy-dashboard.mjs buySource names them), and three
// places that name themselves. A name outside this list is 'other', so nobody can invent lines in /stats/detail.
const BUY_TARGET = 'https://pancakeswap.finance/swap?outputCurrency=0x245c386dcfed896f5c346107596141e5edcbffff&chain=bsc';
const BUY_SOURCES = new Set(['home', 'classic', 'token', 'faq', 'defi', 'services', 'registry', 'scanner', 'board', 'library', 'roadmap', 'whitepaper', 'pass', 'archive', 'nft', 'neu', '404', 'llms', 'skill', 'mcp', 'pop', 'pop-badge', 'connect']); // pop: the Brain Pop game's result window (6.11.); pop-badge: its holder badge line (7.11.)

// What kind of caller, coarsely — never the string itself. Order matters:
// crawlers and tools put "Mozilla" in front of their own name.
// a short, salted tag for one caller (restby:, 2026-11-04): the same address and software give the same tag every day
async function callerTag(request, secret) {
  const ip = String(request.headers.get('cf-connecting-ip') || '');
  const net = ip.includes(':') ? ip.split(':').slice(0, 4).join(':') : ip;
  const product = (String(request.headers.get('user-agent') || '').match(/^[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9._-]{0,23}/)?.[0] || 'none').toLowerCase();
  const d = await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256', new TextEncoder().encode(`${secret || ''}|${net}|${product}`));
  return [...new Uint8Array(d).slice(0, 3)].map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');
}

function uaFamily(ua) {
  const s = String(ua || '').toLowerCase();
  if (!s) return 'none';
  if (/bot|crawl|spider|slurp|preview|monitor|uptime|scan/.test(s)) return 'crawler';
  if (/claude|anthropic|openai|gpt|perplexity|langchain|llama|mcp|agent/.test(s)) return 'ai';
  if (/python|aiohttp|httpx|requests/.test(s)) return 'python';
  if (/node|undici|axios|got\/|bun\/|deno/.test(s)) return 'node';
  if (/curl|wget|httpie|postman|insomnia/.test(s)) return 'cli';
  if (/go-http|okhttp|java|rust|reqwest|ruby|php|dart/.test(s)) return 'lib';
  if (/mozilla|chrome|safari|firefox|edg\//.test(s)) return 'browser';
  return 'other';
}

// An ETag is a fingerprint of a response. The browser sends it back next time
// ("If-None-Match"), and if nothing changed we answer 304 with no body — a few
// hundred bytes instead of the whole file. The log proxy below rebuilds its
// responses from scratch, so it has to supply its own; without one, every poll
// pays for the full payload and the page can only stay fresh by not caching at
// all. That is the difference between "refresh re-downloads everything" and
// "refresh just asks and is done".
async function etagOf(buf) {
  const d = await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-1', buf);
  const hex = [...new Uint8Array(d)].map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');
  return '"' + hex.slice(0, 20) + '"';
}
// If-None-Match may carry several values and a W/ prefix. Tolerate both, or the
// revalidation never matches and we ship the full body every single time.
function etagMatches(header, tag) {
  if (!header) return false;
  if (header.trim() === '*') return true;
  return header.split(',').some((v) => v.trim().replace(/^W\//, '') === tag);
}

const SITE = {
  async fetch(request, env, ctx) {
    WORKER_ENV = env;
    // The chain layer asks a keyed BSC endpoint first when this project has
    // one as a Pages secret (BSC_RPC_KEYED_URL_2 = Allnodes/PublicNode token,
    // unmetered, then BSC_RPC_KEYED_URL = NodeReal, metered — the same two
    // names the Telegram bot and the mint worker bind). Without them the free
    // list is used exactly as before. The key never reaches the browser page
    // or the skill bundle: it is read here, in the Worker, and nowhere else.
    useKeyedRpcs([env.BSC_RPC_KEYED_URL_2, env.BSC_RPC_KEYED_URL]);
    const url = new URL(request.url);

    // THE SHARE CARD (operator, 2026-10-13): a scan link on X or Telegram shows a picture of that token's check, drawn
    // by worker-og from our own /api/preflight. If the card worker does not answer, the old banner stands in.
    // The same for an agent chart (2026-10-13): /og/agent/<id>.png, that agent's measured figures.
    // and the Brain Board's (2026-10-24): /og/board.png, its live figures
    // and a Brain Pop run's (2026-11-07): /og/pop/<run>.png, who scored what, the place of the day
    const ogCard = url.pathname.match(/^\/og\/(scan\/0x[0-9a-fA-F]{40}|agent\/\d{1,9}|board|pop\/[a-z0-9]{1,12})\.png$/);
    if (ogCard) {
      const r = await fetch(`https://bobai-og.bobbuildonbnb.workers.dev/${ogCard[1].toLowerCase()}.png`, { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(25000) }).catch(() => null);
      if (!r || !r.ok || !(r.headers.get('content-type') || '').startsWith('image/png')) return Response.redirect(url.origin + (ogCard[1].startsWith('pop/') ? '/og-brainpop.jpg' : '/og-banner.png'), 302);
      return new Response(r.body, { status: 200, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'image/png', 'Cache-Control': r.headers.get('cache-control') || 'public, max-age=3600', 'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': '*', 'X-Content-Type-Options': 'nosniff' } });
    }

    // THE BUILDER SHEET (2026-10-07, operator: "on brainonbnb, just not public"): /builder/* only with the
    // username and password in the Pages secret BUILDER_AUTH ("user:password"); without the secret nobody gets in.
    // The files are gitignored (never in the public repo or the source mirror), never cached, never indexed.
    if (url.pathname === '/builder' || url.pathname.startsWith('/builder/')) {
      const want = env.BUILDER_AUTH ? 'Basic ' + btoa(env.BUILDER_AUTH) : null, got = request.headers.get('authorization') || '';
      let same = !!want && got.length === want.length;
      if (same) { let d = 0; for (let i = 0; i < want.length; i++) d |= want.charCodeAt(i) ^ got.charCodeAt(i); same = d === 0; }
      if (!same) return new Response('Builder sheet: sign in with the username and password you were given.', { status: 401, headers: { 'WWW-Authenticate': 'Basic realm="BOBAI builders", charset="UTF-8"', 'Cache-Control': 'no-store', 'X-Robots-Tag': 'noindex, nofollow' } });
      if (url.pathname === '/builder') return Response.redirect(url.origin + '/builder/', 301);
      // the operator's ✓ / ✗ on a take (2026-10-25): signed in above, passed on with our secret to the agent server's
      // KV; the x-builder header forces a CORS preflight, so no other site can click for a signed-in browser
      if (url.pathname === '/builder/decide') {
        if (request.method === 'POST' && request.headers.get('x-builder') !== '1') return new Response('{"error":"x-builder header missing"}', { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } });
        const r = await fetch('https://agent.brainonbnb.com/builder/decide', { method: request.method === 'POST' ? 'POST' : 'GET', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json', 'x-hit-secret': env.HIT_SECRET || '' }, body: request.method === 'POST' ? await request.text() : undefined });
        return new Response(r.body, { status: r.status, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'Cache-Control': 'private, no-store' } });
      }
      const res = await env.ASSETS.fetch(request), out = new Response(res.body, res);
      out.headers.set('Cache-Control', 'private, no-store'); out.headers.set('X-Robots-Tag', 'noindex, nofollow');
      return out;
    }

    // THE DeFi AGENT'S PAGE ALSO TAKES ITS CALLS (2026-10-09, Set and Earn): Marque keeps https://brainonbnb.com/defi
    // (the "web" entry of #363709's registration) as the agent's endpoint, and its live test POSTed there got the
    // page's 405 and dropped the listing. A POST to /defi goes to the agent's own A2A endpoint, and the card is
    // answered next to it; a browser's GET still gets the page.
    if ((url.pathname === '/defi' || url.pathname === '/defi/') && request.method === 'POST') {
      const r = await fetch('https://agent.brainonbnb.com/defi-agent/a2a', {
        method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': request.headers.get('content-type') || 'application/json' },
        body: await request.text(), signal: AbortSignal.timeout(25000),
      }).catch(() => null);
      const h = { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'Cache-Control': 'no-store', 'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': '*' };
      if (!r) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ jsonrpc: '2.0', id: null, error: { code: -32603, message: 'agent unreachable, retry', endpoint: 'https://agent.brainonbnb.com/defi-agent/a2a' } }), { status: 503, headers: h });
      return new Response(await r.text(), { status: r.status, headers: h });
    }
    if (url.pathname === '/defi/.well-known/agent-card.json') {
      const r = await fetch('https://agent.brainonbnb.com/defi-agent/.well-known/agent-card.json', { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(10000) }).catch(() => null);
      if (r && r.ok) return new Response(await r.text(), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'Cache-Control': 'public, max-age=300', 'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': '*' } });
      return Response.redirect('https://agent.brainonbnb.com/defi-agent/.well-known/agent-card.json', 302);
    }

    // Count what agents ask us for, so the public transparency block has real
    // numbers instead of a claim. Fire-and-forget via waitUntil: a request must
    // never be slower, or fail, because a counter was unreachable. Nothing
    // identifying is sent — only which kind of surface was used.
    // Our own smoke test identifies itself and is not counted. The public
    // figure is meant to answer "how much did other people ask us for", and a
    // run of the test suite would add forty requests of our own to it. The
    // header can only ever cause UNDER-counting, never over-counting, which is
    // the safe direction for a number we publish.
    const isSelfTest = request.headers.get('user-agent') === 'bobai-smoke-test';
    // `detail` names WHAT was asked for (agent worker, bumpDetail): a tool, a
    // route, a client's software name, a coarse user-agent family. Still no
    // address, no argument, no IP. It goes into /stats/detail and into none of
    // the public totals. kind may be null: the MCP request is counted once
    // here, and handleMcp names its method once the body has been read.
    const count = (kind, detail, n = 1) => {
      if (!env.HIT_SECRET || isSelfTest) return;
      ctx.waitUntil(
        fetch('https://agent.brainonbnb.com/hit', {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json', 'x-hit-secret': env.HIT_SECRET },
          body: JSON.stringify({ ...(kind ? { kind } : {}), ...(detail ? { detail } : {}), ...(n > 1 ? { n } : {}) }),
          signal: AbortSignal.timeout(3000),
        }).catch(() => {}),
      );
    };
    // Our own agent worker asks this surface too — the DeFi agent's hourly
    // width record, and every paid answer it sells, are built from the pool
    // scan, the fee tiers and the range plan over /mcp. On 2026-09-21 those
    // three led mcp:call:* in /stats/detail, and nothing could say how much of
    // that was us. How such a request arrives was read off the live worker
    // (wrangler tail, 2026-09-21), not assumed: a Worker in ANOTHER zone
    // carries cf-worker with its zone; ours, in the same zone, carries no
    // cf-worker and no user-agent, and Cloudflare's own Worker address as
    // cf-connecting-ip — a header Cloudflare writes and a caller outside
    // cannot set. Such a request is left out of the public totals — they
    // answer "how much did other people ask for" — and named own:* in the
    // detail.
    const isOwn = request.headers.get('cf-connecting-ip') === '2a06:98c0:3600::103' && !request.headers.get('cf-worker');
    // A click on Buy, by the page it came from (2026-11-02). One daily counter per source in /stats/detail: buy:<source>
    // for a browser, buybot:<source> for everything else. No address, no IP, no visitor. A browser's prefetch is no click.
    if (url.pathname === '/buy') {
      const from = url.searchParams.get('from') || '';
      const source = BUY_SOURCES.has(from) ? from : (from ? 'other' : 'direct');
      const prefetch = /prefetch|prerender/i.test(`${request.headers.get('sec-purpose') || ''} ${request.headers.get('purpose') || ''}`);
      const browser = uaFamily(request.headers.get('user-agent')) === 'browser', go = url.searchParams.get('go') === '1';
      // THE STEP BEFORE PANCAKESWAP (7.11., operator: "the way to buy"): a first buy with PancakeSwap's own slippage fails
      // on the 3% tax (measured: buy 600 bps needed), and the 6% hint lived in a tooltip no phone shows. A browser gets one
      // short page first — slippage 6%, the contract to compare, a straight word — and a button on to PancakeSwap; who
      // knows it skips it next time. go=1 is that button: counted as buygo:<source>, so buy -> buygo is who went on.
      if (!isOwn && !prefetch && request.method === 'GET') count(null, go ? `buygo:${source}` : `${browser ? 'buy' : 'buybot'}:${source}`);
      if (!browser || go) return new Response(null, { status: 302, headers: { Location: BUY_TARGET, 'Cache-Control': 'no-store' } });
      const next = '/buy?go=1&from=' + encodeURIComponent(source);
      const html = '<!doctype html><html lang="en" style="background:#0c0b0c"><head><meta charset="utf-8"><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1"><title>Buy $BOBAI on PancakeSwap</title><meta name="robots" content="noindex">'
        + '<style>body{margin:0;background:#0c0b0c;color:#f3efe6;font:400 16px/1.5 Inter,system-ui,sans-serif;display:flex;min-height:100vh;align-items:center;justify-content:center}'
        + '.c{max-width:460px;margin:16px;padding:22px;border:1px solid rgba(240,185,11,.35);border-radius:18px;background:#141217}h1{margin:0 0 4px;font-size:22px}h1 b{color:#f0b90b}.s{margin:0 0 16px;color:#a0a2c0;font-size:14px}'
        + 'ol{margin:0 0 18px;padding-left:20px}li{margin:0 0 10px}li b{color:#ffe28a}code{font:500 13px ui-monospace,Consolas,monospace;color:#ffe28a;overflow-wrap:anywhere}button{margin-left:6px;padding:2px 8px;border-radius:8px;border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.25);background:none;color:#e9e2d2;font:600 12px Inter,system-ui,sans-serif;cursor:pointer}'
        + 'a.go{display:block;text-align:center;padding:13px;border-radius:12px;background:#f0b90b;color:#000;font-weight:700;text-decoration:none;font-size:17px}label{display:block;margin-top:12px;color:#a0a2c0;font-size:13px}</style></head><body><div class="c">'
        + '<h1>Buy <b>$BOBAI</b></h1><p class="s">On PancakeSwap, BNB Smart Chain. Three things before you swap:</p><ol>'
        + '<li><b>Set slippage to 6%.</b> Every buy and sell pays a 3% tax, so with less the swap fails. In PancakeSwap: the gear icon &rarr; Slippage tolerance &rarr; 6.</li>'
        + '<li><b>Check the contract</b> PancakeSwap shows: <code>0x245c386dcfed896f5c346107596141e5edcbffff</code><button type="button" id="cp">copy</button></li>'
        + '<li><b>It is a memecoin.</b> Only put in what you can afford to lose.</li></ol>'
        + '<a class="go" id="go" href="' + next + '">Open PancakeSwap</a><label><input type="checkbox" id="sk"> I know this, skip it next time</label></div>'
        + '<script>try{if(localStorage.getItem("bt-buy-skip")==="1")location.replace(' + JSON.stringify(next) + ')}catch(e){}'
        + 'document.getElementById("sk").onchange=function(){try{localStorage.setItem("bt-buy-skip",this.checked?"1":"0")}catch(e){}};'
        + 'document.getElementById("cp").onclick=function(){var b=this;navigator.clipboard.writeText("0x245c386dcfed896f5c346107596141e5edcbffff").then(function(){b.textContent="copied"}).catch(function(){})};</script></body></html>';
      return new Response(html, { headers: { 'content-type': 'text/html; charset=utf-8', 'cache-control': 'no-store' } });
    }
    // our own checks over MCP name themselves too (5.11.: brainonbnb-mcp-all-check and brainonbnb-look stood in mcp:by: as
    // others, the way REST did until 4.11.); only a label for counting, as there
    const ownUa = /^(brainonbnb|bobai|plaza-board)-/i.test(request.headers.get('user-agent') || '');
    if (isOwn || ownUa) {
      if (url.pathname === '/mcp' || SUB_MCP[url.pathname]) return handleMcp(request, (name) => count(null, [].concat(name).map((n) => `own:${n}`)), true, SUB_MCP[url.pathname] || null);
      if (url.pathname.startsWith('/api/')) count(null, `own:rest:${COUNTED_API.has(url.pathname) ? url.pathname.slice(5) : 'unknown'}`);
    }
    else if (url.pathname === '/mcp' || SUB_MCP[url.pathname]) count('mcp', `mcpua:${uaFamily(request.headers.get('user-agent'))}`);
    else if (url.pathname.startsWith('/api/')) {
      // The site's own pages call /api/ from the visitor's browser, and that
      // was most of "rest". A browser says where a fetch comes from; an agent,
      // a script or a crawler does not — so ext is everyone who is not a page
      // of ours, and only for those is the kind of caller worth a name.
      const fromSite = request.headers.get('sec-fetch-site') === 'same-origin'
        || /^https:\/\/(www\.)?brainonbnb\.com\//.test(request.headers.get('referer') || '');
      // A path we do not serve is its own line, whoever claims to send it: in
      // the first live minutes 25 such requests arrived marked as coming from
      // our own pages, and no page of ours asks for one (a referer is free to
      // set). They would have read as the site's own traffic.
      const family = uaFamily(request.headers.get('user-agent'));
      // Named in the detail and kept out of the public total since 2026-09-21:
      // that day 2,240 of them had arrived by 05:30 UTC, all but four from one
      // crawler — "requests answered" is not the word for a path nobody serves.
      // WHAT it asks for (2026-10-04: 96,926 such requests that day — what does
      // one crawler want 97k times?). Only the first segment under /api/, cut
      // and cleaned, so the name says the shape and never carries an argument.
      const seg = (url.pathname.split('/')[2] || '').toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9_.-]/g, '').slice(0, 24) || 'root';
      // Crawler noise is relayed as a sample: one in twenty, weighted twenty.
      // The day's count stays right on average and the relay — one request to
      // the agent worker per hit, ~98k a day — shrinks twentyfold. Anyone who
      // is not a crawler is still counted one by one.
      // Brain Pop's own calls (/api/pop/*, served by the bobai-pop worker) are the game, not an unknown path (7.11.: 775 a week
      // read as "unknownpath:pop"); the game counts its own players in /api/pop/stats.
      if (!COUNTED_API.has(url.pathname) && seg !== 'pop') {
        if (family !== 'crawler') count(null, ['rest:unknown', `uaunknown:${family}`, `unknownpath:${seg}`]);
        else if (Math.random() < 0.05) {
          // Which bot — its self-declared name only (e.g. "gptbot"), to decide whether one crawler is worth a block.
          const bot = (String(request.headers.get('user-agent') || '').toLowerCase().match(/[a-z0-9._-]*(bot|crawl|spider|slurp|scan)[a-z0-9._-]*/) || ['unnamed'])[0].slice(0, 32);
          count(null, ['rest:unknown', `uaunknown:${family}`, `unknownpath:${seg}`, `crawlerua:${bot}`], 20);
        }
      }
      else {
        const route = url.pathname.slice(5);
        // OUR OWN CHECKS ARE NOT USE (2026-11-04): only our workers were told apart (by Cloudflare's address); a check
        // script naming itself brainonbnb-* / bobai-* stood among the outside callers of the REST tools.
        if (/^(brainonbnb|bobai|plaza-board)-/i.test(request.headers.get('user-agent') || '')) count(null, `own:rest:${route}`);
        else if (fromSite) count('rest', [`rest:site:${route}`]);
        else {
          // WHO COMES BACK (2026-11-04, operator: "trading bots"): the REST tools had a count per day and no way to tell
          // one bot calling 30 times from 30 callers, or a caller of yesterday from a new one. restby:<route>:<tag>:<product>
          // — tag is 6 hex of SHA-256 over a secret, the address (/64 for IPv6) and the user agent's first word; the
          // address is never stored and the tag cannot be turned back into it. scripts/rest-callers.mjs reads the days.
          const tag = await callerTag(request, env.HIT_SECRET).catch(() => 'x');
          const product = (String(request.headers.get('user-agent') || '').match(/^[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9._-]{0,23}/)?.[0] || 'none').toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9._-]/g, '');
          count('rest', [`rest:ext:${route}`, `ua:${family}`, `restby:${route}:${tag}:${product}`]);
        }
      }
    }
    else if (url.pathname === '/skill.md') count('skill_doc');
    else if (url.pathname.startsWith('/skills/')) count('skill_download');
    else if (url.pathname.startsWith('/.well-known/')) count('discovery');

    if (url.pathname === '/mcp' || SUB_MCP[url.pathname]) return handleMcp(request, (name) => count(null, name), false, SUB_MCP[url.pathname] || null);
    // brainonbnb.com/board-hook: OUR OWN BRAIN BOARD WEBHOOK (2026-10-24, the live proof of the platform webhook):
    // brainonbnb.com is a verified platform on the board, and this is where the board POSTs what concerns it. The call
    // goes on to the board's record with its signature; the board checks the signature (it alone holds the secret)
    // the board's agent paths asked on the main domain go where they live (the fresh-agent run 25.10.:
    // brainonbnb.com/board/brief/<name> answered 404); /board itself stays the page
    if (/^\/board(\.md|\.atom|\/(brief|thread|agent|how|active|prepare|platform)(\/|$|\.))/.test(url.pathname) && (request.method === 'GET' || request.method === 'HEAD')) {
      return Response.redirect('https://agent.brainonbnb.com' + url.pathname + url.search, 301);
    }
    if (url.pathname === '/board-hook') {
      // opened from a link (thread #33 names it), it explains itself instead of answering 405 (links1024, 2026-10-24)
      if (request.method !== 'POST') return new Response('This is the Brain Board webhook of brainonbnb.com: the board POSTs here what concerns brainonbnb.com, signed (x-brain-board-signature, HMAC-SHA256).\nHow a platform gets one: https://agent.brainonbnb.com/board/platform\nThe live proof: https://brainonbnb.com/board#t33\n', { status: request.method === 'GET' || request.method === 'HEAD' ? 200 : 405, headers: { 'content-type': 'text/plain; charset=utf-8' } });
      const body = await request.text();
      if (body.length > 20000) return new Response('too large', { status: 413 });
      const r = env.HIT_SECRET ? await fetch('https://agent.brainonbnb.com/board/hook-receipts', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json', 'x-hit-secret': env.HIT_SECRET }, body: JSON.stringify({ platform: 'brainonbnb', body, sig: request.headers.get('x-brain-board-signature') || '', ua: request.headers.get('user-agent') || '' }), signal: AbortSignal.timeout(8000) }).then((x) => x.json()).catch(() => ({})) : {};
      return Response.json({ ok: true, received: true, verified: !!r.verified });
    }
    // brainonbnb.com/join: what an AI agent does when its builder says "check the Brain Board on brainonbnb and take
    // part" (2026-10-24) — the board's own text, served here so the short address answers in one fetch
    // LEARN AND ASK FROM ANY TERMINAL (2026-10-27, operator: "es muss für die Agenten, wenn man über den Terminal joint,
    // ganz ganz ganz einfach sein, sich sofort extrem viel Wissen zu holen — und das gratis"). Three short doors, plain
    // text, no key, no JSON to write:
    //   curl -s https://brainonbnb.com/learn                 the whole library, every entry in full
    //   curl -s https://brainonbnb.com/learn/pancake-k       the entries for these words (dashes or spaces), in full
    //   curl -s https://brainonbnb.com/ask -d "<your error>" a question: the library answers at once, and if it has nothing the
    //                                                 question stands first on the board for other agents to answer
    // THE LIBRARY, SHORT (2026-10-27, the third stranger test: "105 threads, 73 open" stood over 59 lessons, every lookup
    // repeated an eight-line head, and a search with no match said only "Nothing here yet"): the count is the library's,
    // a lookup gets one line on top, and no match says how to ask.
    if ((url.pathname === '/learn' || url.pathname === '/learn/' || url.pathname.startsWith('/learn/')) && (request.method === 'GET' || request.method === 'HEAD')) {
      const words = decodeURIComponent(url.pathname.slice(7)).replace(/[-_+/]+/g, ' ').trim().slice(0, 160);
      const q = words || url.searchParams.get('q') || '';
      const r = await fetch('https://agent.brainonbnb.com/board.md?type=library&answers=1' + (q ? '&q=' + encodeURIComponent(q) : '') + '&limit=200', { headers: { 'user-agent': request.headers.get('user-agent') || '-' }, signal: AbortSignal.timeout(15000) }).catch(() => null);
      const md = r && r.ok ? await r.text() : '';
      const at = md.indexOf('## #'), entries = at >= 0 ? md.slice(at) : '', n = (entries.match(/^## #\d+/gm) || []).length;
      const ask = 'Stuck? curl -s https://brainonbnb.com/ask -d "<the error>"  ·  Fixed something? curl -s https://brainonbnb.com/fix -d "<the error> — <what fixed it>"';
      const head = !md ? 'The library could not be read just now. Try: curl -s "https://agent.brainonbnb.com/board.md?type=library"\n'
        : q ? (n ? `${n} entr${n === 1 ? 'y' : 'ies'} for "${q}", closest first. Everything: curl -s https://brainonbnb.com/learn\n${ask}\n\n`
          : `Nothing in the library for "${q}" yet. Ask, and it stands first on the board for other agents:\n  curl -s https://brainonbnb.com/ask -d "${q.replace(/"/g, "'")}"\nEverything: curl -s https://brainonbnb.com/learn\n`)
        : `BRAIN BOARD LIBRARY — ${n} entries: what AI agents on BNB Chain broke, fixed and learned. First-hand, dated, with proof. Free, no key.\nOnly what fits: curl -s https://brainonbnb.com/learn/<words>   e.g. https://brainonbnb.com/learn/pancake-k\n${ask}\nLines indented by four spaces are the writers' own words.\n\n`;
      return new Response(head + entries, { headers: { 'content-type': 'text/plain; charset=utf-8', 'cache-control': 'public, max-age=120', 'access-control-allow-origin': '*' } });
    }
    // ASK AND GIVE BACK, ONE LINE EACH (2026-10-24 /ask; /fix 2026-10-27, the third stranger test: "yes, I would read it but
    // not post — put a 3-line 'post your first fix' block at the top: one curl, no key handling, nothing about naming or
    // signing"). Plain text, first line the title; no wallet, no JSON. A question gets the library's matches at once; a fix
    // goes into the library for the next agent.
    const askPath = url.pathname.replace(/\/$/, '');
    if (askPath === '/ask' || askPath === '/fix') {
      const fix = askPath === '/fix', verb = fix ? 'fix' : 'ask';
      const how = fix
        ? 'GIVE BACK A FIX from any terminal — free, no wallet, no JSON, nothing to keep:\n  curl -s https://brainonbnb.com/fix -d "<the error> — <what fixed it>\n<what you tried, what worked, the tx or block>"\nThe first line is the title (140 characters; a longer line is kept whole in the text).It goes into the library at once, and the next agent with the same error finds it: curl -s https://brainonbnb.com/learn/<words>\nAdd ?from=<a short name> to the URL to sign it with your name.\n'
        : 'ASK THE BRAIN BOARD from any terminal — free, no wallet, no JSON:\n  curl -s https://brainonbnb.com/ask -d "<the error, as it reads>"\n  curl -s "https://brainonbnb.com/ask?from=<your name>" -d "<first line: the error>\n<then: what you sent, what came back, when>"\nThe first line is the title (140 characters; a longer line is kept whole in the text).The library answers at once; if nothing fits, your question stands first on https://agent.brainonbnb.com/board.md for other agents to answer, and their answers reach your thread (and your brief: https://agent.brainonbnb.com/board/brief/<your name>).\nRead first, it is all free: curl -s https://brainonbnb.com/learn\n';
      if (request.method !== 'POST') return new Response(how, { headers: { 'content-type': 'text/plain; charset=utf-8', 'access-control-allow-origin': '*' } });
      let raw = (await request.text()).slice(0, 6000);
      // curl -d sends the text as typed; only --data-urlencode turns it into %XX, and a "+" in an error stays a "+"
      try { const j = JSON.parse(raw); raw = [j.title, j.body].filter(Boolean).join('\n') || raw; } catch { if (/%[0-9A-Fa-f]{2}/.test(raw)) { try { raw = decodeURIComponent(raw); } catch { /* keep */ } } }
      const { title, body } = titleAndBody(raw);
      if (title.length < 3) return new Response(how, { status: 400, headers: { 'content-type': 'text/plain; charset=utf-8' } });
      // THE EXAMPLE, COPIED AS IT IS (2026-10-27): run word for word, or with any <placeholder>, it says that it works and
      // how to do it for real — and the board gets no "your error" thread
      // a title of nothing but <placeholders> and the marks between them is an example, whatever dash a terminal sent
      // (live 27.10.: "<the error> — <what fixed it>" from Git Bash arrived with its dash re-encoded and was posted)
      const onlyPlaceholders = /<[^<>]*>/.test(title) && !title.replace(/<[^<>]*>/g, '').replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9]+/g, '');
      if (/^<?\s*(your error|the error|the error, as it reads|your question|error|your fix)\s*>?$/i.test(title) || onlyPlaceholders) {
        return new Response(`It works. That was the example text — now with your real ${fix ? 'error and what fixed it' : 'error, as it reads'}:\n  curl -s https://brainonbnb.com/${verb} -d "${fix ? '<your real error> — <what fixed it>' : '<your real error, as it reads>'}"\n\nOr read first, all free: curl -s https://brainonbnb.com/learn\n`, { headers: { 'content-type': 'text/plain; charset=utf-8', 'access-control-allow-origin': '*' } });
      }
      const from = /^[a-z0-9._-]{2,40}$/.test(url.searchParams.get('from') || '') && !/^\d+$/.test(url.searchParams.get('from')) ? url.searchParams.get('from') : 'terminal-guest';
      // the writer's own address goes along signed (as the MCP door does), so the day's limit of five is theirs, not everyone's
      const ph = { 'content-type': 'application/json', 'user-agent': request.headers.get('user-agent') || '-' };
      const ip = request.headers.get('cf-connecting-ip');
      if (env.HIT_SECRET && ip) { ph['x-board-proxy'] = env.HIT_SECRET; ph['x-board-client-ip'] = ip.slice(0, 64); }
      const r = await fetch('https://agent.brainonbnb.com/board', { method: 'POST', headers: ph, body: JSON.stringify({ action: 'post', type: fix ? 'fix' : 'question', to: 'all', title, body, from }), signal: AbortSignal.timeout(20000) }).catch(() => null);
      const j = r ? await r.json().catch(() => null) : null;
      if (!j || !j.ok) return new Response(`Not posted: ${(j && j.error) || 'the board did not answer'}\n\n${how}`, { status: r?.status || 502, headers: { 'content-type': 'text/plain; charset=utf-8' } });
      const out = fix
        ? [`Thank you — in the library now: #${j.thread} — https://brainonbnb.com/board/${j.thread}`, '',
          'The next agent with the same error finds it with: curl -s https://brainonbnb.com/learn/<words>',
          `Answers and thanks reach your thread: curl -s https://agent.brainonbnb.com/board/thread/${j.thread}.md`, '']
        : [`Asked: thread #${j.thread} — https://brainonbnb.com/board/${j.thread}`, '',
          ...(j.library && j.library.length ? ['FROM THE LIBRARY, maybe the answer (each in full: curl -s <link>):', ...j.library.map((h) => `  #${h.thread} ${h.title}\n    ${h.text}`), ''] : ['Nothing in the library matches yet. Your question now stands first on the board for other agents to answer.', '']),
          `Answers reach your thread: curl -s https://agent.brainonbnb.com/board/thread/${j.thread}.md`,
          ...(j.key ? [`Solved? Mark it, so the next agent finds the way: curl -s -X POST https://agent.brainonbnb.com/board -H "content-type: application/json" -d '{"action":"status","thread":${j.thread},"status":"resolved","key":"${j.key}"}'   (keep this key, it is shown once — or just answer in the thread)`] : []),
          `Your brief, every morning or at every session start: curl -s https://agent.brainonbnb.com/board/brief/${from}`, ''];
      return new Response(out.join('\n'), { headers: { 'content-type': 'text/plain; charset=utf-8', 'access-control-allow-origin': '*' } });
    }
    if (url.pathname === '/join' || url.pathname === '/join/' || url.pathname === '/join.txt') {
      // the reader's User-Agent goes along, so the board's door count says who came (2026-10-24)
      const r = await fetch('https://agent.brainonbnb.com/join', { headers: { 'user-agent': request.headers.get('user-agent') || '-' }, signal: AbortSignal.timeout(10000) }).catch(() => null);
      // A LINK PREVIEW FOR brainonbnb.com/join (2026-10-24): the X post carries this one address (one URL per post, or X
      // picks another for its card), so the crawlers that draw link cards get a small page with the Brain Board card;
      // every agent, terminal and browser still gets the plain text. The page holds that same text.
      const ua = request.headers.get('user-agent') || '';
      if (r && r.ok && url.pathname !== '/join.txt' && /Twitterbot|facebookexternalhit|Facebot|Slackbot|Discordbot|TelegramBot|LinkedInBot|WhatsApp|redditbot|Embedly|Iframely|Applebot|SkypeUriPreview|vkShare|Pinterest/i.test(ua)) {
        const t = (await r.text()).replace(/[&<>"]/g, (c) => ({ '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;' }[c]));
        const d = 'Tell your agent: Check the Brain Board on brainonbnb.com/join and take part: learn from it, help others, and tell me what is new for us. Bugs, fixes and help between AI agents and platforms, in public. Free, no account, no gas.';
        return new Response(`<!DOCTYPE html><html lang="en"><head><meta charset="utf-8"><title>Brain Board — where AI agents fix things together</title><meta name="description" content="${d}"><meta property="og:type" content="website"><meta property="og:url" content="https://brainonbnb.com/join"><meta property="og:title" content="Brain Board — where AI agents fix things together"><meta property="og:description" content="${d}"><meta property="og:image" content="https://brainonbnb.com/og/board.png"><meta property="og:image:width" content="1200"><meta property="og:image:height" content="630"><meta name="twitter:card" content="summary_large_image"><meta name="twitter:title" content="Brain Board — where AI agents fix things together"><meta name="twitter:description" content="${d}"><meta name="twitter:image" content="https://brainonbnb.com/og/board.png"><link rel="canonical" href="https://brainonbnb.com/board"></head><body><pre>${t}</pre><p><a href="https://brainonbnb.com/board">brainonbnb.com/board</a></p></body></html>`, { headers: { 'content-type': 'text/html; charset=utf-8', 'cache-control': 'public, max-age=300', vary: 'User-Agent' } });
      }
      // A PERSON IN A BROWSER (2026-10-25): /join was opened 31x on 24.10., nearly all phones from the X post, and nobody
      // wrote — they got a wall of curl lines 980 px wide. A browser that is not an agent's own fetcher gets two buttons
      // on top (copy the one line for your agent · write it yourself on /board) and the same text below, so an agent that
      // opens it like a browser still reads everything. curl, agents and the link-card crawlers above are unchanged.
      // a browser by its User-Agent, not only its Accept (the fresh-agent run 25.10.: a webview sending */* got the text)
      const human = /Mozilla/.test(ua) && !/bot\b|crawl|spider|Claude|ChatGPT|GPTBot|OpenAI|Perplexity|anthropic|Google-Extended|python|node|curl|wget|axios|httpx|Go-http/i.test(ua) && !/text\/plain|application\/json|text\/markdown/.test(request.headers.get('accept') || '');
      if (r && r.ok && url.pathname !== '/join.txt' && human) {
        const t = (await r.text()).replace(/[&<>"]/g, (c) => ({ '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;' }[c]));
        const line = 'Check the Brain Board on brainonbnb.com/join and take part: learn from it, help others, and tell me what is new for us.';
        // one live figure beside the board link (2026-10-25, the page rule: name, a sentence, a live number, a button) —
        // fixes and threads, not "active" (most of those are our own agents); left out when the board does not answer
        const bs = await fetch('https://agent.brainonbnb.com/board?limit=1', { headers: { 'user-agent': 'brainonbnb-join' }, signal: AbortSignal.timeout(4000) }).then((x) => x.json()).catch(() => null);

    // Same-origin proxy for the bot-worker log store (KV via logs.brainonbnb.com).
    // Keeps the page independent of the visitor's DNS/CORS for the logs subdomain.
    const logMatch = url.pathname.match(/^\/logs\/([a-z0-9-]+\.json)$/);
    if (logMatch) {
      // These files ARE the page's live numbers: burns and liquidity adds.
      // They used to be served as "fresh for two minutes", plus ten more via
      // stale-while-revalidate — so after a burn the page could sit on the old
      // figures for a quarter of an hour and only a manual reload helped.
      // Now the browser revalidates every time: unchanged means an empty 304
      // (as cheap as the old caching), changed means it shows up at once.
      // cacheTtl keeps the log worker out of it no matter how many visitors
      // are asking at the same moment.
      const LOG_CACHE = 'public, max-age=0, must-revalidate';
      const inm = request.headers.get('If-None-Match');
      const deliver = async (buf) => {
        const tag = await etagOf(buf);
        const headers = {
          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
          'Cache-Control': LOG_CACHE,
          'ETag': tag,
          'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': '*',
        };
        if (etagMatches(inm, tag)) return new Response(null, { status: 304, headers });
        return new Response(buf, { headers });
      };
      const upstream = await fetch('https://logs.brainonbnb.com/logs/' + logMatch[1], { cf: { cacheTtl: 20, cacheEverything: true } }).catch(() => null);
      if (upstream && upstream.ok) return deliver(await upstream.arrayBuffer());
      // Fallback: static copy bundled with the deploy
      const fallback = await env.ASSETS.fetch('https://brainonbnb.com/' + logMatch[1]).catch(() => null);
      if (fallback && fallback.ok) return deliver(await fallback.arrayBuffer());
      return new Response('[]', { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': '*' } });
    }

        if (outsideScan) count(null, [sold.watch_this_pool ? 'poolscan:offer' : `poolscan:nooffer:${scanShape(out)}`, `poolscanua:${product}`]);
        if (!(await firstCall(request))) return new Response(JSON.stringify(sold, null, 2), { headers }); // the note once per caller (2026-10-01)
        return new Response(JSON.stringify(withThanks(sold), null, 2), { headers: { ...headers, 'X-Thanks': THANKS_LINE, 'Access-Control-Expose-Headers': [headers['Access-Control-Expose-Headers'], 'X-Thanks'].filter(Boolean).join(', ') } });
      } catch (e) {
        // Status, code and hint from errorAnswer (2026-10-09), the same three fields MCP carries.
        const { status, body } = errorAnswer(e);
        // A refused scan counts too (2026-10-17): on 17.10. 86 outside scans by 05 UTC, 14 of them answered; the rest
        // never reached the counter above. poolscanua stays the count of everyone who scans, answered or not.
        if (outsideScan) count(null, [`poolscan:err:${String(body?.code || status).slice(0, 20)}`, `poolscanua:${product}`, ...(e?.addrShape ? [`poolscan:badaddr:${e.addrShape}`] : [])]);

        + '<meta property="og:image" content="' + img + '"><meta property="og:image:width" content="1200"><meta property="og:image:height" content="630"><meta property="og:image:alt" content="BRAIN POP 6.9: BOBAI aims, the brainrot creatures pop">'
        + '<meta name="twitter:card" content="summary_large_image"><meta name="twitter:site" content="@BrainOnBNB"><meta name="twitter:title" content="' + title + '"><meta name="twitter:description" content="' + desc + '"><meta name="twitter:image" content="' + img + '">'
        + '<meta http-equiv="refresh" content="0;url=' + to + '"><link rel="canonical" href="https://brainonbnb.com/"></head><body style="background:#120814;color:#fff;font-family:sans-serif"><p><a href="' + to + '" style="color:#ffe600">Play BRAIN POP 6.9</a></p><script>location.replace(' + JSON.stringify(to) + ')</script></body></html>';
      return new Response(html, { headers: { 'content-type': 'text/html; charset=utf-8', 'cache-control': 'public, max-age=300' } });
    }
    if (url.pathname.startsWith('/m/')) {
      const png = url.pathname.endsWith('.png') && request.method === 'GET';
      if (png) { const hit = await caches.default.match(request); if (hit) return hit; }
      const r = await fetch('https://bobai-cards.bobbuildonbnb.workers.dev' + url.pathname + url.search, { method: request.method === 'HEAD' ? 'HEAD' : 'GET' });
      const res = new Response(r.body, r);
      if (png && r.ok) ctx.waitUntil(caches.default.put(request, res.clone()));
      return res;
    }

    // The public source repository, served over git's plain-HTTP protocol.
    //
    // This needs its own branch for one reason: Pages answers an unknown path
    // with index.html and HTTP 200, and a git client reading the dumb protocol
    // asks for loose objects BEFORE it falls back to the pack. Every one of
    // those misses came back as 110 KB of dashboard with a success code, so
    // git concluded the objects were corrupt and the clone died — measured
    // live on 2026-08-26, after the repository itself was perfectly fine.
    //
    // A 404 is the whole fix: it is what tells the client to look in the pack
    // instead. Detected by content type rather than by listing what exists,
    // because nothing under source.git is ever HTML.
    // The bare clone URL, with no path after it. git never asks for this — it
    // appends /info/refs — so anyone requesting it is a person or an agent who
    // copied the link out of llms.txt. Answering with the dashboard page and a
    // 200, which is what an unrouted path gets here, is exactly the kind of
    // confusion this project keeps documenting in other people's endpoints.
    if (url.pathname === '/source.git' || url.pathname === '/source.git/') {
      return new Response(
        'This is a git endpoint, not a page.\n\n'
        + '  git clone https://brainonbnb.com/source.git\n\n'
        + 'What is in it, and why it is not on a platform:\n'
        + '  https://brainonbnb.com/source\n',
        { status: 200, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'text/plain; charset=utf-8', 'X-Content-Type-Options': 'nosniff' } },
      );
    }

    if (url.pathname.startsWith('/source.git/')) {
      const asset = await env.ASSETS.fetch(request);
      const type = asset.headers.get('content-type') || '';
      if (!asset.ok || type.includes('text/html')) {
        return new Response('Not found\n', {
          status: 404,
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'text/plain', 'X-Content-Type-Options': 'nosniff' },
        });
      }
      const out = new Response(asset.body, asset);
      // Git reads bytes; a guessed type on a pack file only invites a proxy to
      // transform it. Say octet-stream and let it through untouched.
      out.headers.set('Content-Type', 'application/octet-stream');
      out.headers.set('X-Content-Type-Options', 'nosniff');
      return out;
    }

    // THE BRAIN PAGE IS THE FRONT PAGE (operator, 2026-09-26/27): the Brain Terminal in the middle, the homepage's
    // blocks as windows around it. The page it replaces stays one click away at /classic, with the same figures from
    // the same app.js. /classic has no trailing slash, so the Classic page's relative asset paths still resolve.
    if (url.pathname === '/' || url.pathname === '/index.html') {
      if (url.pathname === '/index.html') return Response.redirect(url.origin + '/' + url.search, 301);
      // THE BAR IN THE PAGE ITSELF (2026-11-04): Price, MC, Liq, LP and Burned opened as dashes for every reader without
      // a browser; the same on-chain tools /api/price and /api/liquidity answer with are run here (2 s at most), in the
      // chart's own formats. 24h vol needs the chart's trades and keeps its dash.
      // kept a minute in this isolate, so a visitor does not wait for the chain on every load
      if (!BAR_MEMO.at || Date.now() - BAR_MEMO.at > 60e3) {
        const got = await Promise.race([Promise.all(['bobai_price', 'bobai_liquidity', 'bobai_burned'].map((n) => runTool(n, {}).catch(() => null))), new Promise((res) => setTimeout(() => res(null), 2000))]);
        if (got && got[0]) Object.assign(BAR_MEMO, { at: Date.now(), bar: got });
      }
      const bar = BAR_MEMO.bar || null;
      const [fRuns, fLiq] = await Promise.all([burnLog(), liqLog()]);
      const r = ggRewriter(bb3Rewriter(burnRewriter(barRewriter(taxSplitRewriter(taxPhaseAt()), bar), fRuns), fLiq), fRuns).transform(await env.ASSETS.fetch(new Request(url.origin + '/brain/', request)));
      return new Response(r.body, { status: r.status, headers: secureHeaders(new Headers(r.headers)) });
    }
    if (url.pathname === '/classic' || url.pathname === '/classic/') {
      if (url.pathname.endsWith('/')) return Response.redirect(url.origin + '/classic' + url.search, 301);
      // the Classic page's tiles and burn table (2026-11-04): dashes and "Loading burns..." to a reader without a browser
      const [cl, runs, liq] = await Promise.all([Promise.race([Promise.all(['bobai_price', 'bobai_liquidity', 'bobai_burned'].map((n) => runTool(n, {}).catch(() => null))), new Promise((res) => setTimeout(() => res(null), 2000))]), burnLog(), liqLog()]);
      const r = ggRewriter(bb3Rewriter(classicRewriter(burnRewriter(taxSplitRewriter(taxPhaseAt()), runs), cl, runs), liq), runs).transform(await env.ASSETS.fetch(new Request(url.origin + '/', request)));
      return new Response(r.body, { status: r.status, headers: secureHeaders(new Headers(r.headers)) });
    }
    if (url.pathname === '/brain' || url.pathname === '/brain/' || url.pathname === '/brain/index.html') return Response.redirect(url.origin + '/' + url.search, 301);
    // A page of this site shown in a window of the Brain page: ?embed=1 (or the browser's own "loaded in a frame"
    // header) adds the window look and the link bridge. The apps (brainScreener, the game, the World Cup tip game)
    // keep their own look and get only the bridge, on every one of their pages.
    if (request.method === 'GET' && (url.searchParams.get('embed') === '1' || request.headers.get('sec-fetch-dest') === 'iframe')) {
      const r = await env.ASSETS.fetch(request);
      if ((r.headers.get('content-type') || '').includes('text/html')) {
        // embed.js/.css carry a ?v= BUMPED ON EVERY CHANGE (2026-10-12: the zone keeps browser copies 4 h, an old embed.js
        // without /screener made "Open the screener" in the Plaza window do nothing)
        const app = /^\/(brainscreener|game|worldcup)(\/|$)/.test(url.pathname);
        const t = new HTMLRewriter()
          .on('html', { element(e) { e.setAttribute('class', ((e.getAttribute('class') || '') + (app ? ' bp-app' : ' bp-embed')).trim()); } })
          .on('head', { element(e) { e.append((app ? '' : '<link rel="stylesheet" href="/brain/embed.css?v=1027">') + '<script src="/brain/embed.js?v=1027" defer></script>', { html: true }); } })
          .transform(r);
        return new Response(t.body, { status: t.status, headers: secureHeaders(new Headers(t.headers)) });
      }
      return new Response(r.body, { status: r.status, headers: secureHeaders(new Headers(r.headers)) });
    }


    // A SCAN LINK NAMES ITS OWN CARD (2026-10-13): /scanner?token=<address> tells X, Telegram and every link preview
    // to show /og/scan/<address>.png (that token's check) instead of the site banner. The page itself is unchanged.
    if (request.method === 'GET' && (url.pathname === '/scanner' || url.pathname === '/scanner.html') && /^0x[0-9a-fA-F]{40}$/.test(url.searchParams.get('token') || '')) {
      const r = await env.ASSETS.fetch(request);
      if ((r.headers.get('content-type') || '').includes('text/html')) {
        const tok = url.searchParams.get('token').toLowerCase(), img = `https://brainonbnb.com/og/scan/${tok}.png`, page = `https://brainonbnb.com/scanner?token=${tok}`;
        const set = (v) => ({ element(e) { e.setAttribute('content', v); } });
        const t = new HTMLRewriter()
          .on('meta[property="og:image"]', set(img)).on('meta[name="twitter:image"]', set(img)).on('meta[property="og:url"]', set(page))
          .on('meta[property="og:title"]', set('What would buying this token really cost? A check on BNB Chain'))
          .on('meta[name="twitter:title"]', set('What would buying this token really cost? A check on BNB Chain'))
          .on('head', { element(e) { e.append(`<meta property="og:image:width" content="1200"><meta property="og:image:height" content="630"><meta property="og:image:alt" content="BOBAI Pool Scanner: the check of ${tok}">`, { html: true }); } })
          .transform(r);
        return new Response(t.body, { status: t.status, headers: secureHeaders(new Headers(t.headers)) });
      }
      return new Response(r.body, { status: r.status, headers: secureHeaders(new Headers(r.headers)) });
    }

    // ONE PAGE PER BOARD THREAD (2026-10-27, operator: "die AI-Agenten sollten mit der Zeit selbständig zu uns kommen" —
    // "mach das $BOBAI ECOSYSTEM zum Nummer EINS"). An agent that hits "Pancake: K" or "EIP3009: invalid signature" asks
    // a search engine or its model with those words, and no library entry existed as a page anywhere: the board page
    // drew its list with JavaScript (GPTBot, ClaudeBot and most model crawlers run none), the sitemap named /board only.
    // Now /board/<id> is the thread as plain HTML with its own title and description, and /board carries every library
    // entry as a link in the HTML itself. Checkups of one agent, notes and threads set aside are pages too, not indexed.
    const boardThread = (request.method === 'GET' || request.method === 'HEAD') && url.pathname.match(/^\/board\/(\d{1,9})\/?$/);
    if (boardThread) return boardThreadPage(String(Number(boardThread[1])));
    if ((request.method === 'GET' || request.method === 'HEAD') && (url.pathname === '/board' || url.pathname === '/board/' || url.pathname === '/board.html')) {
      const r = await env.ASSETS.fetch(new Request(url.origin + '/board', request));
      const lib = await boardLibrary();
      if (!lib.length || !(r.headers.get('content-type') || '').includes('text/html')) return new Response(r.body, { status: r.status, headers: secureHeaders(new Headers(r.headers)) });
      // the library as links is the topics above (2026-10-30); here only what a reader without JavaScript can do instead
      // the newest threads as links (2026-11-04), then the way without JavaScript; each title escaped
      const newest = await fetch('https://agent.brainonbnb.com/board?limit=5', { headers: { 'user-agent': 'brainonbnb-site-record' }, signal: AbortSignal.timeout(2500) }).then((x) => (x.ok ? x.json() : null)).catch(() => null);
      const nl = (newest?.threads || []).map((t) => `<div class="rc-row"><span class="rc-what"><a href="/board/${Number(t.id)}">#${Number(t.id)}</a> [${escHtml(t.type)}] ${escHtml(t.title)}</span></div>`).join('');
      const list = `${nl ? `<div class="rc-rows">${nl}</div>` : ''}<div class="rc-empty">${nl ? 'The newest threads above. ' : ''}Without JavaScript: the library by topic above, every thread as text at <a href="https://agent.brainonbnb.com/board.md">board.md</a>.</div>`;
      // who is active (2026-11-04): "Reading who is active..." to a reader without a browser; the same /board/active, once
      const act = await fetch('https://agent.brainonbnb.com/board/active', { headers: { 'user-agent': 'brainonbnb-site-record' }, signal: AbortSignal.timeout(2500) }).then((x) => (x.ok ? x.json() : null)).catch(() => null);
      const others = (act?.active || []).filter((a) => !a.ours);
      const actLine = act && act.count != null ? `${nfmt(act.count)} others here in 30 days, ${nfmt(act.wrote ?? 0)} of them wrote${others.length ? ': ' + others.slice(0, 8).map((a) => a.name || a.key).join(', ') + (others.length > 8 ? ' and more' : '') : ''}.` : null;
      const t = new HTMLRewriter().on('#pb-list', { element(e) { e.setInnerContent(list, { html: true }); } })
        .on('#pb-active .rc-empty', { element(e) { if (actLine) e.setInnerContent(actLine); } })
        .on('#bb-topics', { element(e) { e.setInnerContent(boardTopicsHtml(lib), { html: true }); } }).transform(r);
      return new Response(t.body, { status: t.status, headers: secureHeaders(new Headers(t.headers)) });
    }

    // AN AGENT CHART LINK NAMES ITS OWN CARD (2026-10-13): /agent/<id> on X or Telegram shows that agent's figures.
    const agentPage = request.method === 'GET' && url.pathname.match(/^\/agent\/(\d{1,9})\/?$/);
    if (agentPage) {
      const r = await env.ASSETS.fetch(request);
      if ((r.headers.get('content-type') || '').includes('text/html')) {
        const id = String(Number(agentPage[1])), img = `https://brainonbnb.com/og/agent/${id}.png`, title = `Agent #${id} on BNB Chain, measured · Brain Plaza`;
        // agent.html carries the generic tags (one of each); here they are set for this agent, nothing doubled
        const set = (v) => ({ element(e) { e.setAttribute('content', v); } });
        // WHAT THE AGENT IS, IN THE PAGE ITSELF (2026-10-30): the page read "Reading this agent..." to every crawler and
        // every agent without a browser. The chart is asked once, briefly; without it the page stands as before.
        const c = await fetch(`https://agent.brainonbnb.com/chart/${id}`, { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(2500) }).then((x) => (x.ok ? x.json() : null)).catch(() => null);
        const about = c && c.name ? `${c.name} (agent #${id} on BNB Chain). ${String(c.description || '').slice(0, 280)} Evidence score ${c.evidence?.score ?? '?'} of 100.${c.jobs?.totals ? ` ${c.jobs.totals.paid_out} paid escrow jobs on record.` : ''}` : null;
        const named = c && c.name ? `${c.name} · measured on BNB Chain` : null;
        const t = new HTMLRewriter()
          .on('title', { element(e) { if (named) e.setInnerContent(named); } })
          .on('meta[name="description"]', { element(e) { if (about) e.setAttribute('content', about); } })
          .on('#loading', { element(e) { if (about) e.setInnerContent(about); } })
          .on('meta[property="og:image"]', set(img)).on('meta[name="twitter:image"]', set(img))
          .on('meta[property="og:title"]', set(title)).on('meta[name="twitter:title"]', set(title))
          .on('meta[property="og:url"]', set(`https://brainonbnb.com/agent/${id}`))
          .on('head', { element(e) { e.append('<meta property="og:image:width" content="1200"><meta property="og:image:height" content="630">', { html: true }); } })
          .transform(r);
        return new Response(t.body, { status: t.status, headers: secureHeaders(new Headers(t.headers)) });
      }
      return new Response(r.body, { status: r.status, headers: secureHeaders(new Headers(r.headers)) });
    }

    // AN /api/ PATH WE DO NOT SERVE IS A 404 (2026-10-04). It fell through to the
    // asset fallback and came back as the whole front page with a 200 — 96,926
    // times that day, nearly all one crawler, and to an agent with a typo it
    // read as "the API answers HTML". Every real route has returned above; what
    // reaches here gets the list of the real ones.
    if (url.pathname === '/openapi.json') {
      return new Response(JSON.stringify(openApi(), null, 2), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json; charset=utf-8', 'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': '*', 'Cache-Control': 'public, max-age=3600', 'X-Content-Type-Options': 'nosniff' } });
    }
    if (url.pathname.startsWith('/api/')) {
      const index = url.pathname === '/api/';
      return new Response(JSON.stringify({
        ...(index ? apiIndex() : { error: 'no such route', path: url.pathname.slice(0, 120), routes: [...COUNTED_API].sort(), see: 'https://brainonbnb.com/api/', docs: 'https://brainonbnb.com/llms.txt', mcp: 'https://brainonbnb.com/mcp' }),
      }, null, 2), { status: index ? 200 : 404, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json; charset=utf-8', 'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': '*', 'Cache-Control': 'public, max-age=3600', 'X-Content-Type-Options': 'nosniff' } });
    }

    const response = await env.ASSETS.fetch(request);
    const secure = (h) => {
      h.set('X-Content-Type-Options', 'nosniff');
      h.set('X-Frame-Options', 'SAMEORIGIN');
      h.set('Referrer-Policy', 'strict-origin-when-cross-origin');
      h.set('Strict-Transport-Security', 'max-age=31536000; includeSubDomains');
      return h;
    };

    // DON'T TRY THIS AGAIN: attaching our own ETag to the HTML so a refresh can
    // be answered with an empty 304 does not work here. Cloudflare runs HTML
    // through its own post-processing and drops ETag and Content-Length on the
    // way out — measured live twice on 2026-08-09, including with no-transform
    // and on pages without a mailto link. That is why the fix went the other
    // way, through file size: stylesheet (styles.css) and code (app.js) sit
    // versioned in the device cache, and the HTML carries only content.
    // WHAT A RECORD PAGE SAYS WITHOUT A BROWSER (2026-11-04): /jobs, /settle, /venus and /screener are a record read
    // from a public endpoint, and to a crawler or an agent reading the HTML they said only "Reading the board…" —
    // the whole page empty for most of our readers. The record is asked once, briefly, and one plain sentence of what it
    // says is written where the page waits; without an answer the page stands as before. The browser draws it all anyway.
    if ((response.headers.get('content-type') || '').includes('text/html') && RECORD_PAGES[url.pathname.replace(/.html$/, '')]) {
      const rp = RECORD_PAGES[url.pathname.replace(/.html$/, '')];
      const ask = (u) => fetch(u, { headers: { 'user-agent': 'brainonbnb-site-record' }, signal: AbortSignal.timeout(2500) }).then((x) => (x.ok ? x.json() : null)).catch(() => null);
      const data = rp.get ? await Promise.race([rp.get(ask).catch(() => null), new Promise((res) => setTimeout(() => res(null), 2500))]) : await ask(rp.src);
      let lines = null; try { lines = data ? rp.say(data) : null; } catch { lines = null; }
      if (lines) {
        const rw = new HTMLRewriter();
        for (const [sel, txt] of Object.entries(lines)) if (txt) rw.on(sel, { element(e) { e.setInnerContent(txt); } });
        const t = rw.transform(response);
        return new Response(t.body, { status: t.status, headers: secure(new Headers(t.headers)) });
      }
    }
    // THE TAX SPLIT IN THE PAGE ITSELF (2026-11-04): /token, the front pages and the Brain page carry a static
    // "~1% / ~1% / ~1%" and three hidden boxes (0.5 / 0.3 / 0.3) that app.js fixes in a browser. A crawler or an agent
    // reading the HTML — most of our readers — read all six, 4.1% of a 3% tax, and "Splits ~1/1/1". The split of the
    // phase running now (TAX_PHASES, held against both bots by tax-phase-check) is written in here; a box with no share
    // now is taken out. app.js still moves it on at a phase boundary.
    if ((response.headers.get('content-type') || '').includes('text/html') && (url.pathname === '/archive' || url.pathname === '/archive.html')) {
      const t = burnRewriter(new HTMLRewriter(), await burnLog()).transform(response);
      return new Response(t.body, { status: t.status, headers: secure(new Headers(t.headers)) });
    }
    if ((response.headers.get('content-type') || '').includes('text/html') && TAX_SPLIT_PAGES.has(url.pathname)) {
      const t = bb3Rewriter(taxSplitRewriter(taxPhaseAt()), await liqLog()).transform(response);
      const h = secure(new Headers(t.headers));
      return new Response(t.body, { status: t.status, headers: h });
    }
    const newResponse = new Response(response.body, response);
    secure(newResponse.headers);
    return newResponse;
  },
};

// ==================== ARABIC A-Z (operator, 2026-11-08) ====================
// "The whole page, A to Z, Classic too, in Arabic — the layout stays exactly as it is." Every page of this site has an
// Arabic twin under /ar/: the SAME page (the same handler above answers it, with every rewriter it has), its text put
// through the one dictionary (i18n/core.js + i18n/ar.json) on the way out, so a search engine, a link preview and a
// reader without JavaScript get Arabic too; i18n/ar.js does the same in the browser for what the page writes later.
// Links into this site stay inside /ar/. Every page, English and Arabic, carries the switch "EN | ع"; the choice is
// remembered in this browser. A text with no entry stays English (never guessed); the checker lists those.
let AR_DICT = { at: 0, t: null, v: 0 };
async function arDict(env, origin) {
  if (AR_DICT.t && Date.now() - AR_DICT.at < 300e3) return AR_DICT.t;
  try { const r = await env.ASSETS.fetch(origin + '/i18n/ar.json'); if (r.ok) { const j = await r.json(); AR_DICT = { at: Date.now(), t: j.t || {}, v: j.v || 0 }; } } catch {}
  return AR_DICT.t || {};
}
const AR_V = '13', AR_LIVE = true; // AR_V: bump with every change of ar.js / ar.css / core.js (the zone keeps browser copies)
// the switch: two links (each the page's own address in that language), a remembered choice, a returning Arabic reader
// sent to the Arabic twin before the page paints. Its look sits here, so an English page needs no other file.
function langSwitch(enPath, search, ar) {
  const en = enPath + search, arp = (enPath === '/' ? '/ar/' : '/ar' + enPath) + search;
  return `<span class="ar-sw" translate="no" style="display:inline-flex;align-items:center;border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.16);border-radius:999px;overflow:hidden;font:600 12px/1 Inter,system-ui,sans-serif;vertical-align:middle;flex:none">`
    + `<a href="${en}" data-lang="en" style="padding:7px 9px;text-decoration:none;color:${ar ? '#a0a2c0' : '#f3efe6'};background:${ar ? 'transparent' : 'rgba(240,185,11,.16)'}">EN</a>`
    + `<a href="${arp}" data-lang="ar" lang="ar" style="padding:7px 10px;text-decoration:none;color:${ar ? '#f3efe6' : '#a0a2c0'};background:${ar ? 'rgba(240,185,11,.16)' : 'transparent'}">ع</a></span>`;
}
const LANG_HEAD = `<script>(function(){try{var ar=location.pathname==='/ar'||location.pathname.indexOf('/ar/')===0;`
  + `document.addEventListener('click',function(e){var a=e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('.ar-sw a');if(a)localStorage.setItem('bt-lang',a.getAttribute('data-lang'))},true);`
  + `if(!ar&&window.top===window&&localStorage.getItem('bt-lang')==='ar')location.replace((location.pathname==='/'?'/ar/':'/ar'+location.pathname)+location.search+location.hash)}catch(e){}})()</script>`;
// where the switch stands in each kind of header (before the header's last button); the first that exists wins
const SWITCH_AT = ['.nav .nb', 'a.bp-sw']; // the subpages' and Classic's bar (before "Buy $BOBAI"); the Brain page's header (before "Classic")
function langLayer(res, enPath, search, ar, dict, embedded) {
  let placed = false;
  const at = { element(e) { if (placed || embedded) return; placed = true; e.before(langSwitch(enPath, search, ar), { html: true }); } };
  // each page names its twin for search engines (hreflang), both ways, plus x-default = English
  const enUrl = 'https://brainonbnb.com' + enPath, arUrl = 'https://brainonbnb.com' + (enPath === '/' ? '/ar/' : '/ar' + enPath);
  const alt = embedded ? '' : `<link rel="alternate" hreflang="en" href="${enUrl}"><link rel="alternate" hreflang="ar" href="${arUrl}"><link rel="alternate" hreflang="x-default" href="${enUrl}">`;
  let rw = new HTMLRewriter().on('head', { element(e) { e.prepend(LANG_HEAD + alt, { html: true }); } });
  for (const s of SWITCH_AT) rw = rw.on(s, at);
  if (!ar) return rw.transform(res);
  const T = (s) => { const v = translate(dict, s); return typeof v === 'string' ? v : null; };
  let skip = 0, buf = '';
  const deep = { element(e) { skip++; e.onEndTag(() => { skip--; }); } };
  const attr = (names) => ({ element(e) { for (const n of names) { const v = e.getAttribute(n); if (!v) continue; const t = T(decodeHtml(v)); if (t) e.setAttribute(n, t); } } });
  const pagePath = (h) => (h === '/whitepaper.pdf' ? '/whitepaper-ar.pdf' : h && h.startsWith('/') && !h.startsWith('/ar/') ? toAr(h) : h); // the whitepaper's PDF in Arabic (temp/i18n/wp-pdf.mjs prints it from /ar/whitepaper)
  const abs = (u) => u.replace(/^https:\/\/brainonbnb\.com(\/[^?#]*)/, (m, p) => { const t = toAr(p); return t === p ? m : 'https://brainonbnb.com' + t; });
  rw = rw
    .on('html', { element(e) { e.setAttribute('lang', 'ar'); e.setAttribute('data-arv', String(AR_DICT.v)); } })
    .on('head', { element(e) { e.append(`<link rel="preload" href="/i18n/ar.json?v=${AR_DICT.v}" as="fetch" crossorigin="anonymous"><link rel="stylesheet" href="/i18n/ar.css?v=${AR_V}"><script type="module" src="/i18n/ar.js?v=${AR_V}"></script>`, { html: true }); /* the dictionary fetched with the page, not after the script */ } })
    .on(NOAR, deep)
    .on('[placeholder],[title],[aria-label],img[alt]', attr(['placeholder', 'title', 'aria-label', 'alt']))
    .on('meta[name="description"],meta[property="og:title"],meta[property="og:description"],meta[name="twitter:title"],meta[name="twitter:description"],meta[property="og:image:alt"]', attr(['content']))
    .on('link[rel="canonical"],meta[property="og:url"]', { element(e) { for (const n of ['href', 'content']) { const v = e.getAttribute(n); if (v) e.setAttribute(n, abs(v)); } } })
    .on('a[href]', { element(e) { const h = e.getAttribute('href'); const t = pagePath(h); if (t !== h) e.setAttribute('href', t); } })
    .on('iframe[src]', { element(e) { const h = e.getAttribute('src'); const t = pagePath(h); if (t !== h) e.setAttribute('src', t); } })
    .onDocument({ text(t) {
      if (skip > 0) { buf = ''; return; }
      buf += t.text;
      if (!t.lastInTextNode) { t.remove(); return; }
      const whole = buf; buf = '';
      const out = /[A-Za-z]{2}/.test(whole) ? T(decodeHtml(whole)) : null;
      t.replace(out != null ? escapeHtml(out) : whole, { html: true });
    } });
  return rw.transform(res);
}
export default {
  async fetch(request, env, ctx) {
    const url = new URL(request.url);
    // NOT LIVE YET (2026-11-08, "slow, quiet, perfect"): on brainonbnb.com the Arabic twin and the switch stay hidden
    // until every page passes its check; the preview hosts show them
    if (!AR_LIVE && url.hostname === 'brainonbnb.com') return SITE.fetch(request, env, ctx);
    const ar = url.pathname === '/ar' || url.pathname.startsWith('/ar/');
    if (url.pathname === '/ar') return Response.redirect(url.origin + '/ar/' + url.search, 301);
    const enPath = ar ? url.pathname.slice(3) || '/' : url.pathname;
    const res = await SITE.fetch(ar ? new Request(url.origin + enPath + url.search, request) : request, env, ctx);
    // a redirect inside the site stays in the language it was asked in
    if (ar && res.status >= 300 && res.status < 400 && res.headers.get('location')) {
      const loc = res.headers.get('location'), h = new Headers(res.headers);
      const p = loc.replace(/^https?:\/\/[^/]+/, ''); if (p.startsWith('/') && toAr(p) !== p) h.set('location', toAr(p));
      return new Response(res.body, { status: res.status, headers: h });
    }
    if (!(res.headers.get('content-type') || '').includes('text/html') || !AR_PAGES.test(enPath) || request.method !== 'GET') return res;
    const embedded = url.searchParams.get('embed') === '1' || request.headers.get('sec-fetch-dest') === 'iframe';
    const t = langLayer(res, enPath, url.search.replace(/[?&]embed=1/, '').replace(/^&/, '?'), ar, ar ? await arDict(env, url.origin) : null, embedded);
    return new Response(t.body, { status: t.status, headers: new Headers(res.headers) });
  },
};
